\"william_medianish\": (1.81, 3.40, 4.20),\n}\n\ndef proportional(odds):\n    q = [1/o for o in odds]\n    s = sum(q)\n    return [x/s for x in q], s-1\n\ndef additive(odds):\n    q = [1/o for o in odds]\n    m = (sum(q)-1)/3\n    return [x-m for x in q]\n\n# Power method: find k such that sum (1/o)**k = 1\ndef power(odds):\n    lo, hi = 0.5, 2.0\n    for _ in range(60):\n        k = (lo+hi)/2\n        s = sum((1/o)**k for o in odds)\n        if s > 1:\n            lo = k\n        else:\n            hi = k\n    k = (lo+hi)/2\n    ps = [(1/o)**k for o in odds]\n    t = sum(ps)\n    return [p/t proportional(odds)\n    pw, k = power(odds)\n    ad = additive(odds)\n    ps_prop.append(pr)\n    ps_pow.append(pw)\n    print(f\"{name:12} {pr[0]:.3f}   {pw[0]:.3f}   {ad[0]:.3f}   {pr[1]:.3f}   {pw[1]:.3f}   {pr[2]:.3f}  over={over:.3f}\")\n\nimport statistics\nprint(\"mean prop home\", statistics.mean(p[0] for p in ps_prop))\nprint(\"mean pow home\", statistics.mean(p[0] for p in ps_pow))\nprint(\"mean prop no\", statistics.mean(1-p[0] for p in ps_prop))\nprint(\"mean pow no\", statistics.mean(1-p[0] for p in ps_pow))\n\n# EV at ask\ndef ev(p, price):\n    return p*(100/price)
